- **fuse-elementwise-ops**: 2. **Count memory traffic before and after fusion.** For an unfused chain of N operations over a tensor of size S bytes: - Unfused traffic = 2 * N * S (N reads + N writes, ignoring L2 reuse). - Fused traffic = 2 * S (one read pass, one write pass). - Estimate bandwidth saving as (2N - 2) * S. If S is small enough to fit in L2, unfused kernels may already benefit from cache reuse — adjust according ([wenyi-li/awesome-agent-kernel-skills](https://github.com/wenyi-li/awesome-agent-kernel-skills/tree/HEAD/fuse-elementwise-ops) · ★10)
